Relationship Diagram

Table Inventory

_Measures

Every measure in the report, in display folders by subject. Families follow one pattern: X Actual / Budget / Forecast (by month), X Period / Period Budget / Period PY (the selected period), X vs Budget / vs PY, X FY Outlook (actual to date plus forecast).

Columns (1)
ColumnData TypeSort BySummarizeFormatStatus
Valueint64none0Hidden
As Of Date

Last month end with actuals. Every period ends here.

Folder: 0 Dates & Period   Format: mmm d, yyyy

CALCULATE( MAX( Sales[Date] ), 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", REMOVEFILTERS() )

Columns: Sales[Date], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: None detected

Period Selected

The period picked on the Period buttons (YTD when none).

Folder: 0 Dates & Period   Format: 

SELECTEDVALUE( Period[Period], "YTD" )

Columns: Period[Period]

Measures: None detected

Window End

Last day of the selected period: the last month end with actuals.

Folder: 0 Dates & Period   Format: Short Date

[As Of Date]

Columns: None detected

Measures: As Of Date

Window Start

First day of the selected period.

Folder: 0 Dates & Period   Format: Short Date

VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
VAR _MonthStart = DATE( YEAR( _AsOf ), MONTH( _AsOf ), 1 )
RETURN
    SWITCH(
        [Period Selected],
        "Month", _MonthStart,
        "QTD", DATE( YEAR( _AsOf ), ( QUARTER( _AsOf ) - 1 ) * 3 + 1, 1 ),
        "Last 12M", EDATE( _MonthStart, -11 ),
        DATE( YEAR( _AsOf ), 1, 1 )
    )

Columns: None detected

Measures: As Of Date, Period Selected

FY Start

Folder: 0 Dates & Period   Format: Short Date

Columns: None detected

Net Revenue FY Outlook

Net Revenue. Full year: actual to date plus forecast for the rest of the year.

Folder: 1 Income Statement   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
RETURN
    CALCULATE( [Net Revenue], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], [FY Start], _AsOf ) )
        + CALCULATE( [Net Revenue], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Forecast", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], _AsOf + 1, [FY End] ) )

Columns: Date[Date], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: As Of Date, FY End, FY Start, Net Revenue

Net Revenue FY Budget

Net Revenue. Full-year budget.

Folder: 1 Income Statement   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

CALCULATE( [Net Revenue], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Budget", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], [FY Start], [FY End] ) )

Columns: Date[Date], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: FY End, FY Start, Net Revenue

Net Revenue Next FY Forecast

Net Revenue. Forecast for next year.

Folder: 1 Income Statement   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

CALCULATE( [Net Revenue], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Forecast", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], [FY End] + 1, EDATE( [FY End], 12 ) ) )

Columns: Date[Date], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: FY End, Net Revenue

Net Revenue Actual Line

Net Revenue. Actual, for trend lines (blank after the last month of actuals).

Folder: 1 Income Statement   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

IF( MAX( 'Date'[Date] ) <= [As Of Date], [Net Revenue Actual] )

Columns: Date[Date]

Measures: As Of Date, Net Revenue Actual

Net Revenue Forecast Line

Net Revenue. Forecast, for trend lines (starts at the last month of actuals so the lines join).

Folder: 1 Income Statement   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

VAR _D = MAX( 'Date'[Date] )
VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
RETURN
    SWITCH( TRUE(), _D = _AsOf, [Net Revenue Actual], _D > _AsOf, [Net Revenue Forecast] )

Columns: Date[Date]

Measures: As Of Date, Net Revenue Actual, Net Revenue Forecast

Net Revenue Budget Line

Net Revenue. Budget, for trend lines.

Folder: 1 Income Statement   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

IF( NOT ISBLANK( [Net Revenue Budget] ), [Net Revenue Budget] )

Columns: None detected

Measures: Net Revenue Budget

EBITDA FY Outlook

EBITDA. Full year: actual to date plus forecast for the rest of the year.

Folder: 1 Income Statement   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
RETURN
    CALCULATE( [EBITDA], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], [FY Start], _AsOf ) )
        + CALCULATE( [EBITDA], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Forecast", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], _AsOf + 1, [FY End] ) )

Columns: Date[Date], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: As Of Date, EBITDA, FY End, FY Start

EBITDA. Forecast, for trend lines (starts at the last month of actuals so the lines join).

Folder: 1 Income Statement   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

VAR _D = MAX( 'Date'[Date] )
VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
RETURN
    SWITCH( TRUE(), _D = _AsOf, [EBITDA Actual], _D > _AsOf, [EBITDA Forecast] )

Columns: Date[Date]

Measures: As Of Date, EBITDA Actual, EBITDA Forecast

CapEx Spend FY Outlook

CapEx Spend. Full year: actual to date plus forecast for the rest of the year.

Folder: 7 CapEx   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
RETURN
    CALCULATE( [CapEx Spend], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], [FY Start], _AsOf ) )
        + CALCULATE( [CapEx Spend], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Forecast", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], _AsOf + 1, [FY End] ) )

Columns: Date[Date], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: As Of Date, CapEx Spend, FY End, FY Start

Folder: 4 Balance Sheet & Working Capital   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

CALCULATE( SUM( Inventory[Inventory Value] ), LASTDATE( 'Date'[Date] ) )

Columns: Date[Date], Inventory[Inventory Value]

Measures: None detected

Aged Inventory

Inventory on hand more than 180 days.

Folder: 4 Balance Sheet & Working Capital   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

CALCULATE( SUM( Inventory[Aged 180+ Days] ), LASTDATE( 'Date'[Date] ) )

Columns: Date[Date], Inventory[Aged 180+ Days]

Measures: None detected

Aged Inventory %

Inventory on hand more than 180 days, as a share of inventory.

Folder: 4 Balance Sheet & Working Capital   Format: 0.0%;-0.0%

DIVIDE( [Aged Inventory], [Inventory Detail] )

Columns: None detected

Measures: Aged Inventory, Inventory Detail

Commercial DSO

Days sales outstanding for commercial accounts: receivables over the last 3 months of their sales, times 91.

Folder: 4 Balance Sheet & Working Capital   Format: 0

VAR _D = MAX( 'Date'[Date] )
VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
VAR _Sales3M =
    IF(
        SELECTEDVALUE( 'Scenario'[Scenario] ) = "Forecast",
        CALCULATE( [Sales Net], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ), 'Date'[Date] > _AsOf, Customers[Customer Type] = "Commercial" )
            + CALCULATE( [Sales Net], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ), 'Date'[Date] <= _AsOf, 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", Customers[Customer Type] = "Commercial" ),
        CALCULATE( [Sales Net], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ), Customers[Customer Type] = "Commercial" )
    )
RETURN
    DIVIDE( [Commercial Receivables], _Sales3M ) * 91

Columns: Customers[Customer Type], Date[Date], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: As Of Date, Commercial Receivables, Sales Net

DIO

Days inventory outstanding: inventory over the last 3 months of product cost, times 91.

Folder: 4 Balance Sheet & Working Capital   Format: 0

VAR _D = MAX( 'Date'[Date] )
VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
VAR _Cost3M =
    IF(
        SELECTEDVALUE( 'Scenario'[Scenario] ) = "Forecast",
        CALCULATE( [Sales Cost], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ), 'Date'[Date] > _AsOf, KEEPFILTERS( Products[Category] <> "Services" ) )
            + CALCULATE( [Sales Cost], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ), 'Date'[Date] <= _AsOf, 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", KEEPFILTERS( Products[Category] <> "Services" ) ),
        CALCULATE( [Sales Cost], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ), KEEPFILTERS( Products[Category] <> "Services" ) )
    )
RETURN
    DIVIDE( [Inventory Detail], _Cost3M ) * 91

Columns: Date[Date], Products[Category], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: As Of Date, Inventory Detail, Sales Cost

DPO

Days payables outstanding: payables over the last 3 months of cost of sales, times 91.

Folder: 4 Balance Sheet & Working Capital   Format: 0

VAR _D = MAX( 'Date'[Date] )
VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
VAR _Cost3M =
    IF(
        SELECTEDVALUE( 'Scenario'[Scenario] ) = "Forecast",
        CALCULATE( [Sales Cost], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ), 'Date'[Date] > _AsOf )
            + CALCULATE( [Sales Cost], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ), 'Date'[Date] <= _AsOf, 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual" ),
        CALCULATE( [Sales Cost], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ) )
    )
RETURN
    DIVIDE( [Payables Balance], _Cost3M ) * 91

Columns: Date[Date], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: As Of Date, Payables Balance, Sales Cost

DSO

Days sales outstanding, all revenue (consumer card sales settle in two days).

Folder: 4 Balance Sheet & Working Capital   Format: 0

VAR _D = MAX( 'Date'[Date] )
VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
VAR _Rev3M =
    IF(
        SELECTEDVALUE( 'Scenario'[Scenario] ) = "Forecast",
        CALCULATE( [Net Revenue], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ), 'Date'[Date] > _AsOf )
            + CALCULATE( [Net Revenue], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ), 'Date'[Date] <= _AsOf, 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual" ),
        CALCULATE( [Net Revenue], DATESINPERIOD( 'Date'[Date], _D, -3, MONTH ) )
    )
RETURN
    DIVIDE( [Receivables Balance], _Rev3M ) * 91

Columns: Date[Date], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: As Of Date, Net Revenue, Receivables Balance

Liquidity Floor Line

The liquidity floor, drawn on liquidity charts.

Columns: None detected

Lowest Forecast Liquidity

Lowest month-end liquidity in the next 6 forecast months.

Folder: 4 Balance Sheet & Working Capital   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
VAR _Months =
    CALCULATETABLE( VALUES( 'Date'[Month End] ), DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], _AsOf + 1, EOMONTH( _AsOf, 6 ) ) )
RETURN
    MINX( _Months, VAR _D = 'Date'[Month End] RETURN CALCULATE( [Liquidity Forecast], 'Date'[Date] = _D ) )

Columns: Date[Date], Date[Month End]

Measures: As Of Date, Liquidity Forecast

Lowest Forecast Liquidity Month

Month of the lowest forecast liquidity.

Folder: 4 Balance Sheet & Working Capital   Format: 

VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
VAR _Months =
    ADDCOLUMNS(
        CALCULATETABLE( VALUES( 'Date'[Month End] ), DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], _AsOf + 1, EOMONTH( _AsOf, 6 ) ) ),
        "@Liq", VAR _D = 'Date'[Month End] RETURN CALCULATE( [Liquidity Forecast], 'Date'[Date] = _D )
    )
RETURN
    FORMAT( MAXX( TOPN( 1, _Months, [@Liq], ASC ), 'Date'[Month End] ), "mmm yyyy" )

Columns: Date[Date], Date[Month End]

Measures: As Of Date, Liquidity Forecast

P&L Line Value

Value of the income statement row: costs negative, margins as a share of revenue.

Folder: 6 Statements   Format: #,0

VAR _Keys = SELECTEDVALUE( 'P&L Layout'[Accounts] )
VAR _Den = SELECTEDVALUE( 'P&L Layout'[Denominator] )
VAR _Num =
    CALCULATE( SUM( 'General Ledger'[Signed Amount] ), FILTER( ALL( Accounts[AccountKey] ), PATHCONTAINS( _Keys, Accounts[AccountKey] ) ) )
RETURN
    IF(
        SELECTEDVALUE( 'P&L Layout'[Kind] ) = "Ratio",
        DIVIDE( _Num, CALCULATE( SUM( 'General Ledger'[Signed Amount] ), FILTER( ALL( Accounts[AccountKey] ), PATHCONTAINS( _Den, Accounts[AccountKey] ) ) ) ),
        _Num
    )

Columns: Accounts[AccountKey], General Ledger[Signed Amount], P&L Layout[Accounts], P&L Layout[Denominator], P&L Layout[Kind]

Measures: None detected

P&L vs Budget

Actual less budget: positive is better (more revenue or less cost). Margins in points.

Folder: 6 Statements   Format: 

VAR _V = [P&L Actual] - [P&L Budget]
RETURN IF( SELECTEDVALUE( 'P&L Layout'[Kind] ) = "Ratio", _V * 100, _V )

Columns: P&L Layout[Kind]

Measures: P&L Actual, P&L Budget

P&L vs Budget %

Variance as a share of budget.

Folder: 6 Statements   Format: +0.0%;-0.0%;–

Columns: P&L Layout[Kind]

Measures: P&L Actual, P&L Budget

Columns: P&L Layout[Kind]

P&L FY Outlook

Full-year outlook: actual to date plus forecast ($K).

Folder: 6 Statements   Format: 

VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
VAR _Keys = SELECTEDVALUE( 'P&L Layout'[Accounts] )
VAR _Den = SELECTEDVALUE( 'P&L Layout'[Denominator] )
VAR _Num = ( CALCULATE( SUM( 'General Ledger'[Signed Amount] ), FILTER( ALL( Accounts[AccountKey] ), PATHCONTAINS( _Keys, Accounts[AccountKey] ) ), 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], [FY Start], _AsOf ) ) + CALCULATE( SUM( 'General Ledger'[Signed Amount] ), FILTER( ALL( Accounts[AccountKey] ), PATHCONTAINS( _Keys, Accounts[AccountKey] ) ), 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Forecast", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], _AsOf + 1, [FY End] ) ) )
RETURN
    IF( SELECTEDVALUE( 'P&L Layout'[Kind] ) = "Ratio", DIVIDE( _Num, ( CALCULATE( SUM( 'General Ledger'[Signed Amount] ), FILTER( ALL( Accounts[AccountKey] ), PATHCONTAINS( _Den, Accounts[AccountKey] ) ), 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], [FY Start], _AsOf ) ) + CALCULATE( SUM( 'General Ledger'[Signed Amount] ), FILTER( ALL( Accounts[AccountKey] ), PATHCONTAINS( _Den, Accounts[AccountKey] ) ), 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Forecast", DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], _AsOf + 1, [FY End] ) ) ) ), _Num )

Columns: Accounts[AccountKey], Date[Date], General Ledger[Signed Amount], P&L Layout[Accounts], P&L Layout[Denominator], P&L Layout[Kind], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: As Of Date, FY End, FY Start

Revenue Bridge Value

Revenue variance to budget split into volume (units at the budget average price), mix (which product lines sold), price and markdowns beyond the budgeted rate. The steps add up to actual less budget.

Folder: 8 Variance Bridges   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

VAR _T =
    ADDCOLUMNS(
        VALUES( Products[ProductKey] ),
        "@UA", [Sales Units Period], "@UB", [Sales Units Period Budget],
        "@NA", [Sales Net Period], "@NB", [Sales Net Period Budget],
        "@GA", [Sales Gross Period], "@DA", [Sales Discounts Period],
        "@GB", [Sales Gross Period Budget], "@DB", [Sales Discounts Period Budget]
    )
VAR _UA = SUMX( _T, [@UA] )
VAR _UB = SUMX( _T, [@UB] )
VAR _NA = SUMX( _T, [@NA] )
VAR _NB = SUMX( _T, [@NB] )
VAR _AvgB = DIVIDE( _NB, _UB )
VAR _AtBudgetPrice = SUMX( _T, IF( [@UB] > 0, [@UA] * DIVIDE( [@NB], [@UB] ), [@NA] ) )
VAR _Volume = ( _UA - _UB ) * _AvgB
VAR _Mix = _AtBudgetPrice - _UA * _AvgB
VAR _Markdowns = - ( SUMX( _T, [@DA] ) - SUMX( _T, [@GA] * DIVIDE( [@DB], [@GB] ) ) )
VAR _Price = ( _NA - _AtBudgetPrice ) - _Markdowns
RETURN
    SWITCH( SELECTEDVALUE( 'Revenue Bridge'[Step] ), "Volume", _Volume, "Mix", _Mix, "Price", _Price, "Markdowns", _Markdowns, _NA - _NB )

Columns: Products[ProductKey], Revenue Bridge[Step]

Measures: Sales Discounts Period, Sales Discounts Period Budget, Sales Gross Period, Sales Gross Period Budget, Sales Net Period, Sales Net Period Budget, Sales Units Period, Sales Units Period Budget

EBITDA Bridge Value

EBITDA variance to budget by driver: sales volume and mix at the budget margin, markdowns beyond budget (net of the cost they would have carried), product cost and price, freight and obsolescence beyond the budget rate, then each cost line. The steps add up to actual less budget.

Folder: 8 Variance Bridges   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

VAR _RA = [Net Revenue Period]
VAR _RB = [Net Revenue Period Budget]
VAR _GMB = DIVIDE( [Gross Margin Period Budget], _RB )
VAR _GSA = [Gross Sales Period]
VAR _GSB = [Gross Sales Period Budget]
VAR _DA = [Discounts & Markdowns Period]
VAR _DB = [Discounts & Markdowns Period Budget]
VAR _FA = [Freight & Obsolescence Period]
VAR _FB = [Freight & Obsolescence Period Budget]
VAR _Volume = ( _RA - _RB ) * _GMB
VAR _Markdowns = - ( _DA - _GSA * DIVIDE( _DB, _GSB ) ) * ( 1 - _GMB )
VAR _Freight = - ( _FA - _RA * DIVIDE( _FB, _RB ) )
VAR _GMVar = [Gross Margin Period] - [Gross Margin Period Budget]
VAR _Product = _GMVar - _Volume - _Markdowns - _Freight
RETURN
    SWITCH(
        SELECTEDVALUE( 'EBITDA Bridge'[Step] ),
        "Sales volume & mix", _Volume,
        "Markdowns", _Markdowns,
        "Product cost & price", _Product,
        "Freight & obsolescence", _Freight,
        "Labor", - [Labor Cost vs Budget],
        "Marketing", - [Marketing Cost vs Budget],
        "Technology & admin", - [Technology & Admin Cost vs Budget],
        "Occupancy", - [Occupancy Cost vs Budget],
        [EBITDA vs Budget]
    )

Columns: EBITDA Bridge[Step]

Measures: Discounts & Markdowns Period, Discounts & Markdowns Period Budget, EBITDA vs Budget, Freight & Obsolescence Period, Freight & Obsolescence Period Budget, Gross Margin Period, Gross Margin Period Budget, Gross Sales Period, Gross Sales Period Budget, Labor Cost vs Budget, Marketing Cost vs Budget, Net Revenue Period, Net Revenue Period Budget, Occupancy Cost vs Budget, Technology & Admin Cost vs Budget

Cash Bridge Value

Cash bridge: opening cash, then each source and use of cash in the selected period. The total bar is closing cash.

Folder: 8 Variance Bridges   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

VAR _Start = EOMONTH( [Window Start], -1 )
VAR _Step = SELECTEDVALUE( 'Cash Bridge'[Step] )
VAR _End = [Window End]
VAR _Win = CALCULATETABLE( VALUES( 'Date'[Date] ), DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], [Window Start], [Window End] ) )
RETURN
    SWITCH(
        _Step,
        "Opening cash", CALCULATE( [Cash Balance], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", 'Date'[Date] = _Start ),
        "Net income", CALCULATE( [Cash Flow Amount], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", _Win, 'Cash Flow Lines'[Bridge Group] = "Net Income" ),
        "D&A (non-cash)", CALCULATE( [Cash Flow Amount], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", _Win, 'Cash Flow Lines'[Bridge Group] = "Depreciation & Amortization" ),
        "Receivables", CALCULATE( [Cash Flow Amount], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", _Win, 'Cash Flow Lines'[Cash Flow Line] = "Change in Receivables" ),
        "Inventory", CALCULATE( [Cash Flow Amount], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", _Win, 'Cash Flow Lines'[Cash Flow Line] = "Change in Inventory" ),
        "Payables", CALCULATE( [Cash Flow Amount], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", _Win, 'Cash Flow Lines'[Cash Flow Line] = "Change in Payables" ),
        "Other working capital", CALCULATE( [Cash Flow Amount], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", _Win, 'Cash Flow Lines'[Cash Flow Line] IN { "Change in Prepaid & Other", "Change in Accrued Liabilities", "Change in Gift Cards & Deferred Revenue" } ),
        "CapEx", CALCULATE( [Cash Flow Amount], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", _Win, 'Cash Flow Lines'[Bridge Group] = "Capital Expenditures" ),
        "Debt, net", CALCULATE( [Cash Flow Amount], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", _Win, 'Cash Flow Lines'[Bridge Group] = "Debt" ),
        "Distributions", CALCULATE( [Cash Flow Amount], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", _Win, 'Cash Flow Lines'[Bridge Group] = "Distributions" ),
        CALCULATE( [Cash Balance], 'Scenario'[Scenario] = "Actual", 'Date'[Date] = _End )
    )

Columns: Cash Bridge[Step], Cash Flow Lines[Bridge Group], Cash Flow Lines[Cash Flow Line], Date[Date], Scenario[Scenario]

Measures: Cash Balance, Cash Flow Amount, Window End, Window Start

Customers for 80% of Revenue

How many accounts make up 80% of commercial revenue.

Folder: 9 Customers & Products   Format: 0

VAR _T = ADDCOLUMNS( CALCULATETABLE( VALUES( Customers[CustomerKey] ), Customers[Customer Type] = "Commercial", REMOVEFILTERS( Customers ) ), "@R", [Sales Net Period] )
VAR _Tot = SUMX( _T, [@R] )
RETURN
    COUNTROWS( FILTER( _T, VAR _R = [@R] RETURN SUMX( FILTER( _T, [@R] > _R ), [@R] ) < 0.8 * _Tot ) )

Columns: Customers[Customer Type], Customers[CustomerKey]

Measures: Sales Net Period

Customer Cumulative %

Pareto: running share of revenue from the largest account down to this one.

Folder: 9 Customers & Products   Format: 0.0%;-0.0%

VAR _Cur = [Sales Net Period]
VAR _T = ADDCOLUMNS( ALL( Customers[Customer] ), "@R", [Sales Net Period] )
RETURN
    IF( NOT ISBLANK( _Cur ), DIVIDE( SUMX( FILTER( _T, [@R] >= _Cur ), [@R] ), SUMX( _T, [@R] ) ) )

Columns: Customers[Customer]

Measures: Sales Net Period

Customer AR Rank

Rank of the account by commercial receivables at the last month end.

Folder: 9 Customers & Products   Format: 0

VAR _AR = [Commercial Receivables As Of]
RETURN
    IF( NOT ISBLANK( _AR ) && _AR > 0, RANKX( FILTER( CALCULATETABLE( VALUES( Customers[CustomerKey] ), Customers[Customer Type] = "Commercial", REMOVEFILTERS( Customers ) ), [Commercial Receivables As Of] > 0 ), [Commercial Receivables As Of], _AR, DESC ) )

Columns: Customers[Customer Type], Customers[CustomerKey]

Measures: Commercial Receivables As Of

Customer Pareto Subtitle

Customer Pareto subtitle.

    "Top 5 accounts: " & FORMAT( [Top 5 Customer Share], "0%" ) & " of commercial revenue (policy limit 45%)  ·  " & [Customers for 80% of Revenue] & " of " & [Commercial Accounts Active] & " accounts make 80%  ·  largest: " & [Largest Customer] & " " & FORMAT( [Largest Customer Share], "0%" ),
    REMOVEFILTERS( Customers )
)

Columns: None detected

Measures: Commercial Accounts Active, Customers for 80% of Revenue, Largest Customer, Largest Customer Share, Top 5 Customer Share

Product Pareto Subtitle

Product Pareto subtitle.

Folder: 9 Customers & Products   Format: 

VAR _T = ADDCOLUMNS( ALL( Products[Product Line] ), "@M", [Product Margin Period] )
VAR _Tot = SUMX( _T, [@M] )
VAR _N = COUNTROWS( FILTER( _T, VAR _R = [@M] RETURN SUMX( FILTER( _T, [@M] > _R ), [@M] ) < 0.8 * _Tot ) )
RETURN
    _N & " of " & COUNTROWS( _T ) & " product lines make 80% of product margin  ·  thinnest margin: " & MAXX( TOPN( 1, FILTER( ADDCOLUMNS( ALL( Products[Product Line] ), "@P", [Product Margin % Period] ), NOT ISBLANK( [@P] ) ), [@P], ASC ), Products[Product Line] ) & " " & FORMAT( MINX( FILTER( ADDCOLUMNS( ALL( Products[Product Line] ), "@P", [Product Margin % Period] ), NOT ISBLANK( [@P] ) ), [@P] ), "0%" )

Columns: Products[Product Line]

Measures: Product Margin % Period, Product Margin Period

Product Margin Cumulative %

Pareto: running share of product margin from the most profitable line down to this one.

Folder: 9 Customers & Products   Format: 0.0%;-0.0%

VAR _Cur = [Product Margin Period]
VAR _T = ADDCOLUMNS( ALLSELECTED( Products[Product Line] ), "@M", [Product Margin Period] )
RETURN
    IF( NOT ISBLANK( _Cur ), DIVIDE( SUMX( FILTER( _T, [@M] >= _Cur ), [@M] ), SUMX( _T, [@M] ) ) )

Columns: Products[Product Line]

Measures: Product Margin Period

Completion Date

Schedule Slip (Months)

Months late (positive) or early.

Folder: 7 CapEx   Format: +0;-0;0

VAR _Plan = MAX( 'CapEx Projects'[Planned Completion] )
VAR _Done = [Completion Date]
RETURN
    IF( NOT ISBLANK( _Plan ) && NOT ISBLANK( _Done ), ( YEAR( _Done ) - YEAR( _Plan ) ) * 12 + MONTH( _Done ) - MONTH( _Plan ) )

Columns: CapEx Projects[Planned Completion]

Measures: Completion Date

Project Status

Complete, In progress or Planned.

Folder: 7 CapEx   Format: 

VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
RETURN
    SWITCH(
        TRUE(),
        NOT ISBLANK( MAX( 'CapEx Projects'[Actual Completion] ) ), "Complete",
        [Spent to Date] > 0, "In progress",
        "Planned"
    )

Columns: CapEx Projects[Actual Completion]

Measures: As Of Date, Spent to Date

Project RAG

On track, Watch (over budget by 2%+ or a month late) or Act now (10%+ over or 3+ months late).

Folder: 7 CapEx   Format: 

VAR _Over = [Over Budget %]
VAR _Slip = [Schedule Slip (Months)]
RETURN
    SWITCH( TRUE(), _Over > 0.10 || _Slip >= 3, "Act now", _Over > 0.02 || _Slip >= 1, "Watch", "On track" )

Columns: None detected

Measures: Over Budget %, Schedule Slip (Months)

CapEx Cumulative Outlook

Capital spend so far this year with the forecast after the last month of actuals.

Folder: 7 CapEx   Format: $#,0;($#,0)

VAR _AsOf = [As Of Date]
VAR _D = MAX( 'Date'[Date] )
RETURN
    IF( _D >= _AsOf, CALCULATE( [CapEx Spend Actual], DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], DATE( YEAR( _D ), 1, 1 ), _AsOf ) ) + CALCULATE( [CapEx Spend Forecast], DATESBETWEEN( 'Date'[Date], _AsOf + 1, _D ) ) )

Columns: Date[Date]

Measures: As Of Date, CapEx Spend Actual, CapEx Spend Forecast

        "Gross margin %", 0.003,
        "EBITDA", 0.05,
        "EBITDA margin %", 0.005,
        "Labor % of revenue", 0.003,
        "Revenue per labor hour", 0.02,
        "Markdown rate", 0.003,
        "Operating cash flow", 0.1,
        "Commercial DSO (days)", 3,

KPI Gap

How far the KPI is on the good side of target (negative = behind): share of target for money and rates, absolute for % and days.

Folder: 10 Scorecard & Summary   Format: 0.000

VAR _A = [KPI Actual]
VAR _T = [KPI Target]
VAR _Gap = IF( [KPI Higher Is Better] = 1, _A - _T, _T - _A )
RETURN
    IF( [KPI Kind] IN { "money", "rate" }, DIVIDE( _Gap, ABS( _T ) ), _Gap )

Columns: None detected

Measures: KPI Actual, KPI Higher Is Better, KPI Kind, KPI Target

KPI Actual Text

Folder: 10 Scorecard & Summary   Format: 

VAR _V = [KPI Actual]
RETURN
    IF(
        ISBLANK( _V ), BLANK(),
        SWITCH(
            [KPI Kind],
            "money", IF( ABS( ( _V ) ) >= 1000000, FORMAT( ( _V ) / 1000000, "$#,0.0M;-$#,0.0M" ), FORMAT( ( _V ) / 1000, "$#,0K;-$#,0K" ) ),
            "pct", FORMAT( _V, "0.0%" ),
            "days", FORMAT( _V, "0" ) & " days",
            "rate", FORMAT( _V, "$#,0" ),
            FORMAT( _V, "#,0.0" )
        )
    )

KPI vs Target Text

Actual less target, formatted.

Folder: 10 Scorecard & Summary   Format: 

VAR _A = [KPI Actual]
VAR _T = [KPI Target]
VAR _D = _A - _T
RETURN
    IF(
        NOT ISBLANK( _A ) && NOT ISBLANK( _T ),
        SWITCH(
            [KPI Kind],
            "money", IF( _D >= 0, "+", "-" ) & IF( ABS( ( ABS( _D ) ) ) >= 1000000, FORMAT( ( ABS( _D ) ) / 1000000, "$#,0.0M;-$#,0.0M" ), FORMAT( ( ABS( _D ) ) / 1000, "$#,0K;-$#,0K" ) ),
            "pct", FORMAT( _D * 100, "+0.0;-0.0" ) & " pts",
            "days", FORMAT( _D, "+0;-0" ) & " days",
            "rate", FORMAT( _D, "+$#,0;-$#,0" ),
            FORMAT( _D, "+#,0.0;-#,0.0" )
        )
    )

Columns: None detected

Measures: KPI Actual, KPI Kind, KPI Target

KPI vs PY Text

Change on last year, formatted.

Folder: 10 Scorecard & Summary   Format: 

VAR _A = [KPI Actual]
VAR _P = [KPI PY]
RETURN
    IF(
        NOT ISBLANK( _A ) && NOT ISBLANK( _P ),
        SWITCH(
            [KPI Kind],
            "pct", FORMAT( ( _A - _P ) * 100, "+0.0;-0.0" ) & " pts",
            "days", FORMAT( _A - _P, "+0;-0" ) & " days",
            FORMAT( DIVIDE( _A - _P, ABS( _P ) ), "+0.0%;-0.0%" )
        )
    )

Columns: None detected

Measures: KPI Actual, KPI Kind, KPI PY

    "R4", [Aged Inventory % As Of],

Risk Indicator Text

The indicator, formatted.

Folder: 12 Risks   Format: 

VAR _V = [Risk Indicator Value]
VAR _ID = SELECTEDVALUE( 'Risk Register'[Risk ID] )
RETURN
    SWITCH( _ID, "R1", IF( ABS( ( _V ) ) >= 1000000, FORMAT( ( _V ) / 1000000, "$#,0.0M;-$#,0.0M" ), FORMAT( ( _V ) / 1000, "$#,0K;-$#,0K" ) ), "R3", FORMAT( _V, "0" ) & " days", "R7", FORMAT( _V, "+0%;-0%" ), FORMAT( _V, "0.0%" ) )

Columns: Risk Register[Risk ID]

Measures: Risk Indicator Value

Risk Threshold Text

Where the risk turns red.

Folder: 12 Risks   Format: 

VAR _ID = SELECTEDVALUE( 'Risk Register'[Risk ID] )
VAR _R = SELECTEDVALUE( 'Risk Register'[Red At] )
RETURN
    "Red " & IF( SELECTEDVALUE( 'Risk Register'[Higher Is Worse] ) = 1, "above ", "below " ) &
    SWITCH( _ID, "R1", IF( ABS( ( _R ) ) >= 1000000, FORMAT( ( _R ) / 1000000, "$#,0.0M;-$#,0.0M" ), FORMAT( ( _R ) / 1000, "$#,0K;-$#,0K" ) ), "R3", FORMAT( _R, "0" ) & " days", "R7", FORMAT( _R, "0%" ), FORMAT( _R, "0.0%" ) )

Columns: Risk Register[Higher Is Worse], Risk Register[Red At], Risk Register[Risk ID]

Measures: None detected

Risk Level

3 red, 2 amber, 1 green.

Folder: 12 Risks   Format: 0

VAR _V = [Risk Indicator Value]
VAR _A = SELECTEDVALUE( 'Risk Register'[Amber At] )
VAR _R = SELECTEDVALUE( 'Risk Register'[Red At] )
VAR _Worse = SELECTEDVALUE( 'Risk Register'[Higher Is Worse] ) = 1
RETURN
    IF( ISBLANK( _V ), BLANK(), IF( _Worse, SWITCH( TRUE(), _V >= _R, 3, _V >= _A, 2, 1 ), SWITCH( TRUE(), _V <= _R, 3, _V <= _A, 2, 1 ) ) )

Columns: Risk Register[Amber At], Risk Register[Higher Is Worse], Risk Register[Red At]

Measures: Risk Indicator Value

Risk Likelihood

Likelihood 1-5 from the indicator: 5 well past red, 4 red, 3 amber, 2 green.

Folder: 12 Risks   Format: 0

VAR _V = [Risk Indicator Value]
VAR _R = SELECTEDVALUE( 'Risk Register'[Red At] )
VAR _Worse = SELECTEDVALUE( 'Risk Register'[Higher Is Worse] ) = 1
VAR _Far = IF( _Worse, _V >= _R * 1.2, _V <= _R * 0.9 )
RETURN
    SWITCH( [Risk Level], 3, IF( _Far, 5, 4 ), 2, 3, 1, 2 )

Columns: Risk Register[Higher Is Worse], Risk Register[Red At]

Measures: Risk Indicator Value, Risk Level

Risk Heat Cell

Risk IDs that sit in this cell of the heat map.

Folder: 12 Risks   Format: 

VAR _I = SELECTEDVALUE( 'Risk Impact'[Impact] )
VAR _L = SELECTEDVALUE( 'Risk Likelihood'[Likelihood] )
VAR _Risks =
    FILTER( ALL( 'Risk Register'[Risk ID], 'Risk Register'[Impact] ), 'Risk Register'[Impact] = _I && [Risk Likelihood] = _L )
RETURN
    CONCATENATEX( _Risks, 'Risk Register'[Risk ID], "  ", 'Risk Register'[Risk ID], ASC ) & ""

Columns: Risk Impact[Impact], Risk Likelihood[Likelihood], Risk Register[Impact], Risk Register[Risk ID]

Measures: Risk Likelihood

Net revenue Card

Card with comparison columns: the whole card as one picture, 300 x 140 pixels. Colors come from the KPI Visualizer; change them there and paste the script again.

Folder: KPI Visuals\Net revenue   Format: 

VAR _Value = [Net Revenue Period]
VAR _ValueText =
	SWITCH (
		TRUE (),
		ISBLANK ( _Value ), "",
		ABS ( _Value ) >= 1E9, FORMAT ( _Value / 1E9, "\$#,0.0" ) & "bn",
		ABS ( _Value ) >= 1E6, FORMAT ( _Value / 1E6, "\$#,0.0" ) & "M",
		ABS ( _Value ) >= 1E3, FORMAT ( _Value / 1E3, "\$#,0.0" ) & "K",
		FORMAT ( _Value, "\$#,0" )
	)
VAR _Target = [Net Revenue Period Budget]
VAR _VarT = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _Value ) && NOT ISBLANK ( _Target ) && _Target <> 0, DIVIDE ( _Value - _Target, ABS ( _Target ) ) )
VAR _ColT =
	SWITCH (
		TRUE (),
		ISBLANK ( _VarT ), "#5B6B7C",
		_VarT >= 0.02 && _VarT > 0, "#006100",
		_VarT <= -0.02 && _VarT < 0, "#9C0006",
		"#9C5700"
	)
VAR _VarTText = IF ( ISBLANK ( _VarT ), "", SWITCH ( TRUE (), _VarT > 0, UNICHAR ( 9650 ), _VarT < 0, UNICHAR ( 9660 ), UNICHAR ( 9658 ) ) & " " & FORMAT ( _VarT, "+0.0%;-0.0%;0.0%" ) )
VAR _TargetText =
	SWITCH (
		TRUE (),
		ISBLANK ( _Target ), "",
		ABS ( _Target ) >= 1E9, FORMAT ( _Target / 1E9, "\$#,0.0" ) & "bn",
		ABS ( _Target ) >= 1E6, FORMAT ( _Target / 1E6, "\$#,0.0" ) & "M",
		ABS ( _Target ) >= 1E3, FORMAT ( _Target / 1E3, "\$#,0.0" ) & "K",
		FORMAT ( _Target, "\$#,0" )
	)
VAR _Compare = [Net Revenue Period PY]
VAR _VarC = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _Value ) && NOT ISBLANK ( _Compare ) && _Compare <> 0, DIVIDE ( _Value - _Compare, ABS ( _Compare ) ) )
VAR _ColC =
	SWITCH (
		TRUE (),
		ISBLANK ( _VarC ), "#5B6B7C",
		_VarC >= 0.02 && _VarC > 0, "#006100",
		_VarC <= -0.02 && _VarC < 0, "#9C0006",
		"#9C5700"
	)
VAR _VarCText = IF ( ISBLANK ( _VarC ), "", SWITCH ( TRUE (), _VarC > 0, UNICHAR ( 9650 ), _VarC < 0, UNICHAR ( 9660 ), UNICHAR ( 9658 ) ) & " " & FORMAT ( _VarC, "+0.0%;-0.0%;0.0%" ) )
VAR _CompareText =
	SWITCH (
		TRUE (),
		ISBLANK ( _Compare ), "",
		ABS ( _Compare ) >= 1E9, FORMAT ( _Compare / 1E9, "\$#,0.0" ) & "bn",
		ABS ( _Compare ) >= 1E6, FORMAT ( _Compare / 1E6, "\$#,0.0" ) & "M",
		ABS ( _Compare ) >= 1E3, FORMAT ( _Compare / 1E3, "\$#,0.0" ) & "K",
		FORMAT ( _Compare, "\$#,0" )
	)
VAR _Svg =
	"<svg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' width='300' height='140' viewBox='0 0 300 140'>"
		& "<defs><clipPath id='kvc'><rect width='300' height='140' rx='10'/></clipPath></defs>"
		& "<rect x='0.5' y='0.5' width='299' height='139' rx='10' fill='#FFFFFF' stroke='#E3E7EC'/>"
		& "<rect width='6' height='140' fill='#F5A800' clip-path='url(#kvc)'/>"
		& "<text x='20' y='28' font-family='Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif' font-size='13' fill='#5B6B7C'>Net revenue</text>"
		& "<text x='20' y='60' font-family='Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif' font-size='28' fill='#01305C' font-weight='600'>" & _ValueText & "</text>"
		& "<line x1='20' y1='76' x2='284' y2='76' stroke='#E3E7EC'/>"
		& "<text x='20' y='96' font-family='Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif' font-size='11' fill='#5B6B7C'>vs budget</text><text x='20' y='116' font-family='Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif' font-size='15' fill='" & _ColT & "' font-weight='600'>" & _VarTText & "</text><text x='20' y='132' font-family='Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif' font-size='11' fill='#5B6B7C'>Budget " & _TargetText & "</text>"
		& "<line x1='146' y1='86' x2='146' y2='132' stroke='#E3E7EC'/><text x='152' y='96' font-family='Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif' font-size='11' fill='#5B6B7C'>vs last year</text><text x='152' y='116' font-family='Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif' font-size='15' fill='" & _ColC & "' font-weight='600'>" & _VarCText & "</text><text x='152' y='132' font-family='Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif' font-size='11' fill='#5B6B7C'>Last year " & _CompareText & "</text>"
		& "</svg>"
RETURN
	IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _Value ), "data:image/svg+xml;utf8," & SUBSTITUTE ( SUBSTITUTE ( _Svg, "%", "%25" ), "#", "%23" ) )

Columns: None detected

Measures: Net Revenue Period, Net Revenue Period Budget, Net Revenue Period PY

VAR _VarTText = IF ( ISBLANK ( _VarT ), "", SWITCH ( TRUE (), _VarT > 0, UNICHAR ( 9650 ), _VarT < 0, UNICHAR ( 9660 ), UNICHAR ( 9658 ) ) & " " & FORMAT ( _VarT, "+0.0%;-0.0%;0.0%" ) )
VAR _TargetText =
	SWITCH (
		TRUE (),
		ISBLANK ( _Target ), "",
		ABS ( _Target ) >= 1E9, FORMAT ( _Target / 1E9, "\$#,0.0" ) & "bn",
		ABS ( _Target ) >= 1E6, FORMAT ( _Target / 1E6, "\$#,0.0" ) & "M",
		ABS ( _Target ) >= 1E3, FORMAT ( _Target / 1E3, "\$#,0.0" ) & "K",
		FORMAT ( _Target, "\$#,0" )
	)

	SWITCH (
		TRUE (),
		ISBLANK ( _VarC ), "#5B6B7C",

	SWITCH (
		TRUE (),

Impactint64none0Financial impact if it happens, 1 (low) to 5 (severe).
IndicatorstringnoneKey risk indicator.
Amber AtdoublenoneHiddenIndicator value where the risk turns amber.
Red AtdoublenoneHiddenIndicator value where the risk turns red.
Higher Is Worseint64none0Hidden1 when a higher indicator is worse.
ResponsestringnoneMitigation.

Sales

Monthly sales by location, product line and customer, for Actual, Budget and Forecast. Revenue and cost of sales in the general ledger are these rows summed.

Columns (10)
ColumnData TypeSort BySummarizeFormatStatusDescription
DatedateTimenoneShort DateHiddenMonth end. Links to Date.
ScenariostringnoneHiddenActual, Budget or Forecast. Links to Scenario.
LocationKeyint64none0HiddenLinks to Locations.
ProductKeyint64none0HiddenLinks to Products.
CustomerKeyint64none0HiddenLinks to Customers.
Unitsint64sum#,0Units sold (sessions or hours for services).
Gross Salesdoublesum#,0Units at the price charged, before discounts.
Discountsdoublesum#,0Discounts, markdowns and trade discounts.
Net Salesdoublesum#,0Gross sales less discounts.
Cost of Salesdoublesum#,0Product cost (or service delivery cost) of the units.

Scenario

Actual, Budget or Forecast. Filters every fact table.

Columns (2)
ColumnData TypeSort BySummarizeFormatStatusDescription
Scenariostring'Scenario Order'noneActual, Budget or Forecast.
Scenario Orderint64none0HiddenSort order.
